│ def eprint(*args, **kwargs): print(*args, file=sys.stderr, **kwargs)         │
│                                                                              │
│ def guess_target_name(model_file, initial_dim, target_dim): """ Given a      │
│ model name with the convention a.<dim>.b, this function returns the model's  │
│ name with target_dim value. For example model_file name cc.en.300.bin with   │
│ initial dim 300 becomes cc.en.100.bin when the target_dim is 100. """ prg =  │
│ re.compile("(.).%s.(.)" % initial_dim) m = prg.match(model_file) if m:       │
│ return "%s.%d.%s" % (m.group(1), target_dim, m.group(2))                     │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  sp_ext = os.path.splitext(model_file)                                       │
│  return &quot;%s.%d%s&quot; % (sp_ext[0], target_dim, sp_ext[1])             │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ def command_reduce(model_file, target_dim, if_exists): """ Given a           │
│ model_file, this function reduces its dimension to target_dim by applying a  │
│ PCA. """ eprint("Loading model")                                             │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  ft = fasttext.load_model(model_file)                                        │
│  initial_dim = ft.get_dimension()                                            │
│  if target_dim &gt;= initial_dim:                                            │
│      raise Exception(&quot;Target dimension (%d) should be less than         │
│  initial dimension (%d).&quot; % (                                           │
│          target_dim, initial_dim))                                           │
│                                                                              │
│  result_filename = guess_target_name(model_file, initial_dim, target_dim)    │
│  if os.path.isfile(result_filename):                                         │
│      if if_exists == &#x27;overwrite&#x27;:                                  │
│          pass                                                                │
│      elif if_exists == &#x27;strict&#x27;:                                   │
│          raise Exception(                                                    │
│              &quot;File already exists. Use --overwrite to                   │
│  overwrite.&quot;)                                                           │
│      elif if_exists == &#x27;ignore&#x27;:                                   │
│          return result_filename                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  eprint(&quot;Reducing matrix dimensions&quot;)                              │
│  fasttext.util.reduce_model(ft, target_dim)                                  │
│                                                                              │
│  eprint(&quot;Saving model&quot;)                                            │
│  ft.save_model(result_filename)                                              │
│  eprint(&quot;%s saved&quot; % result_filename)                              │
│                                                                              │
│  return result_filename                                                      │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ def main(): global args                                                      │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(                                           │
│      description=&#x27;fastText helper tool to reduce model                  │
│  dimensions.&#x27;)                                                          │
│  parser.add_argument(&quot;model&quot;, type=str,                            │
│                      help=&quot;model file to reduce. model.bin&quot;)       │
│  parser.add_argument(&quot;dim&quot;, type=int,                              │
│                      help=&quot;targeted dimension of word vectors.&quot;)   │
│  parser.add_argument(&quot;--overwrite&quot;,                                │
│  action=&quot;store_true&quot;,                                              │
│                      help=&quot;overwrite if file exists.&quot;)             │
│                                                                              │
│  args = parser.parse_args()                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│  command_reduce(args.model, args.dim, if_exists=(                            │
│      &#x27;overwrite&#x27; if args.overwrite else &#x27;strict&#x27;))       │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ if name == 'main': main() </output>                                          │
